Our framework is comprehensively evaluated on six distinct datasets spanning industrial, enterprise, general-purpose, smart home, and military automation domains across 44 transfer scenarios. Experimental results demonstrate strong zero-shot generalization in several cross-domain settings and competitive performance against a contrastive domain-adaptation baseline under realistic, heterogeneous, and privacy-constrained IoT conditions.

</details>


### [226] [LongCounsel-8: A Benchmark Suite for Longitudinal Depression Tracking from Multi-Session Counseling Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03507)
*Jiayi Li,Zhaomin Wu,Bingsheng He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LongCounsel-8，一个包含7,749条五会话咨询轨迹的抑郁症纵向跟踪基准数据集，用于评估从多会话咨询对话中跟踪抑郁症状严重程度及其跨会话变化的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从多会话咨询对话中跟踪抑郁症需要同时估计当前症状严重程度及跨会话变化，但缺乏具有标准化会话级抑郁标签的纵向咨询数据。现有资源要么只有无标签的多会话对话，要么只有单会话的有标签访谈，限制了该任务的研究进展。

Method: 构建包含三个独立生成数据集的基准LongCounsel-8。采用三种关键策略：(1)基于真实客户画像的仿真模拟确保纵向一致性和多样性；(2)基于经验数据的构建确保症状进展符合实证模式；(3)间接行为实现确保抑郁状态表达自然且不直接暴露目标标签。

Result: 模拟自报告能够紧密恢复受控状态，支持标签保真度。实验发现三点关键结论：(1)较低的单会话得分误差不保证趋势（改善或恶化）识别准确；(2)现有方法在恶化轨迹上一致性可靠性较低；(3)额外会话历史可能降低趋势预测准确度。

Conclusion: LongCounsel-8为抑郁症评估从静态单会话预测向可靠的纵向心理健康变化跟踪发展奠定了基础。

Abstract: Tracking depression from multi-session counseling dialogues requires estimating both current symptom severity and how it changes across sessions. Yet progress on this task is constrained by the scarcity of longitudinal counseling data with standardized session-level depression labels. Existing resources typically provide either multi-session conversations without depression labels or labeled interviews in a single session. Building such a benchmark poses three challenges: maintaining longitudinal consistency and diversity, grounding symptom progression in empirical patterns, and expressing controlled depression states naturally without exposing target labels. To address these challenges, we introduce LongCounsel-8, a benchmark suite of three independently generated datasets totaling 7,749 five-session counseling trajectories, grounded in real-world client profiles, depression trajectories, symptom compositions, and counseling patterns. We combine profile-grounded simulation, empirically informed state construction, and indirect behavioral realization to address these challenges. Across the benchmark, simulated self-reports closely recover the controlled states, supporting label fidelity. Experiments on existing depression tracking methods reveal three key findings: (1) lower single-session score error does not guarantee accurate identification of trend, i.e., improvement or worsening; (2) existing methods are consistently less reliable on worsening trajectories; and (3) additional session history may reduce the accuracy of trend prediction. Together, these findings establish LongCounsel-8 as a foundation for advancing depression assessment from static, single-session prediction toward reliable longitudinal tracking of mental-health change.

</details>


### [227] [LeanGRPO: Eliminating Redundant Recomputation in Diffusion RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03528)
*Sijie Wang,Zhiqiang Tan,Xinrui Yang,Shaohuai Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LeanGRPO通过重构数据并行布局并引入两种免重算的训练调度（LeanGRPO-Retain和LeanGRPO-Reweight），消除了扩散强化学习中rollout与更新阶段的前馈骨干网络重复计算，在FLUX.1-dev和Wan上实现最高1.83倍端到端加速且不改变原始优化目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散强化学习方法（如DanceGRPO、FlowGRPO）在rollout后需要对选中时间步进行带梯度追踪的重算，这在同后端、同策略训练下是数学上冗余的。直观上rollout和策略更新可复用同一前馈骨干网络，但直接复用会带来rollout阶段巨大的内存开销，因此需要设计免重算的高效训练方案。

Method: LeanGRPO通过重构数据并行布局，并提出两种免重算训练调度：(1) LeanGRPO-Retain：rollout期间启用梯度追踪，直接复用计算图和保存的激活值用于update阶段反向传播，无需重算；(2) LeanGRPO-Reweight：rollout期间也启用梯度，但用临时优势值立即对每个选中步反向传播并延迟梯度同步，待轨迹完成后用真实优势值修正临时梯度。两种调度分别针对不同模型规模和输入尺寸设计。

Result: 在FlowGRPO/DanceGRPO配合FLUX.1-dev和Wan的实验中，LeanGRPO实现了最高1.83倍的端到端加速，同时保持了原始优化目标不变。

Conclusion: LeanGRPO证明了扩散强化学习中rollout与更新阶段的前馈计算可以安全复用而不牺牲优化目标，通过合理的内存管理和梯度调度策略显著提升训练效率，为大规模扩散生成模型的后训练提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion reinforcement learning (RL) has recently achieved significant success in post-training image and video generative models. However, most diffusion RL methods, including DanceGRPO and FlowGRPO, recompute selected timesteps with gradient tracking after rollout. Under on-policy training with the same backend for rollout and update, this recomputation is mathematically redundant. Intuitively, the rollout and policy update steps can reuse the same feed-forward backbone to avoid redundant computation, but doing so can incur a large memory overhead during rollout. To address the issue, we present LeanGRPO by restructuring the data-parallel layout and introducing two recompute-free training schedules for trajectory-logprob diffusion RL: (1) LeanGRPO-Retain enables gradient tracking during rollout and directly reuses the resulting computation graphs and saved activations for backward during update, requiring no recomputation; and (2) LeanGRPO-Reweight also enables gradients during rollout, but immediately backpropagates each selected step using a provisional advantage and delays gradient synchronization, then corrects the provisional gradients with the true advantage after the trajectory is completed. These schedules target different model scales and input sizes. Across FlowGRPO/DanceGRPO with FLUX.1-dev and Wan, LeanGRPO achieves up to 1.83x end-to-end speedup while preserving the original optimization objective.

</details>


### [228] [Coupled Scaling: A Representational Accessibility Framework for Neural Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03533)
*Jie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"耦合缩放"(Coupled Scaling)框架，认为神经网络的有限预算缩放指数不仅取决于数据几何或数据-模型谱，还取决于任务结构与架构-优化系统可触及几何之间的关系。通过模式截断模型推导出残差指数介于[ρ·γ, γ]区间，并给出了任务加权谱度量下的训练时间指数表达式，最后提出两个可检验的预测及对涌现轨迹的审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经缩放理论要么从数据几何出发，要么从指定的数据-模型谱出发推导缩放律，但忽略了同一数据在不同架构或优化设置下可能产生不同缩放行为这一事实。作者希望建立一个任务条件化框架，将架构-优化系统的可达几何与任务结构的关系纳入缩放律的推导。

Method: 作者引入Coupled Scaling框架：(1) 在可解的模式截断模型中，损失分解为架构支撑外的目标能量和未解决的支撑尾两部分；(2) 对任意优先级顺序，残差介于最优N支撑尾和已完成高值前缀之外的最大尾之间；(3) 推导出残差指数落在[ρ_{A,O,T}γ_{A,T}, γ_{A,T}]区间；(4) 在有限偏移前缀增益下，前缀完成速率起决定作用，α=ργ；(5) 固定核特例中，从独立于损失拟合的任务加权谱度量的近零尾推导训练时间指数。

Result: 推导出残差指数区间为[ρ_{A,O,T}γ_{A,T}, γ_{A,T}]，在限定条件下α_{A,O,T}=ρ_{A,O,T}γ_{A,T}=ρ_{A,O,T}(b_{A,T}-1)。提出了两个可检验的预测：静态任务相关几何应在共同预算下跟踪损失，而多尺度几何应跟踪耦合特定指数排序，包括跨对比任务的反转。并对已发布的涌现轨迹进行审计，识别出直接阶乘测试所需的控制变量。

Conclusion: 神经网络缩放不应仅被看作数据属性的函数，而是任务结构、架构可达几何与优化获取过程三者耦合的结果。该框架将架构支撑与有限预算获取分离，为设计直接检验缩放机制（而非仅拟合缩放曲线）的实验提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Existing theories derive neural scaling from data geometry or a specified data-model spectrum, but systems trained on the same data can scale differently when architecture or optimization changes the representations they can efficiently reach. We introduce Coupled Scaling, a task-conditioned framework in which finite-budget scaling depends on the relation between task structure and the geometry accessible to an architecture-optimization system. In a solvable mode-truncation model, loss separates into target energy outside architectural support and an unresolved supported tail. For an arbitrary priority order, the residual lies between the best-N supported tail and the tail beyond the largest completed high-value prefix. If the cumulative-tail and coverage log-rates are $γ_{A,T}$ and $ρ_{A,O,T}$, the residual exponent lies in $[ρ_{A,O,T}γ_{A,T},γ_{A,T}]$. Under bounded off-prefix gain, the completed prefix is rate-determining and $α_{A,O,T}=ρ_{A,O,T}γ_{A,T}$; for $a_{A,T,j}\asymp j^{-b_{A,T}}$, this gives $α_{A,O,T}=ρ_{A,O,T}(b_{A,T}-1)$. A fixed-kernel specialization derives the training-time exponent from the near-zero tail of a task-weighted spectral measure defined independently of the loss fit. The framework separates architectural support from finite-budget acquisition and motivates two tests: static task-relevant geometry should track loss at a common budget, while multiscale geometry should track coupling-specific exponent ordering, including reversal across contrasting tasks. An audit of released emergence trajectories identifies the controls needed for a direct factorial test that measures geometry separately from the scaling fit.

</details>


### [229] [WeatherNext 3: Increasing resolution and performance of global weather models with raw observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03582)
*Stephan Rasp,Boris Babenko,Dominic Masters,Andrew El-Kadi,Samier Merchant,Guy Shalev,Ilan Price,Fred Zyda,Remi Lam,Sasha Shysheya,Matthew Willson,Stratis Markou,Shreya Agrawal,Suhani Vora,Mohammed Alewi Hassen,Sunny Mak,Tom R. Andersson,Megan Bela,Akib Uddin,Nofar Peled Levi,Ben Gaiarin,Ferran Alet,Aaron Bell,Peter Battaglia,Alvaro Sanchez-Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WeatherNext 3 是新一代AI天气预测模型，通过每小时生成预报、接入低延迟静止卫星数据、达到与物理模型相当的时空分辨率（小时级/0.1度），并学习预测卫星降水、热带气旋及站点观测，实现了对传统"数据同化-预报-后处理"三阶段流水线的超越，建立了概率中程预报的新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的AI天气模型存在两个关键缺陷：1) 时空分辨率低于最佳物理模型；2) 仅使用分析数据初始化和训练，无法直接利用观测数据，且会继承分析数据的偏差。这限制了AI模型的应用范围与预测精度。

Method: 1) 每小时生成新预报（而非传统全球模型的每6小时），通过摄入低延迟地球静止卫星数据；2) 时间步长小时级、单层变量空间分辨率0.1度（含太阳辐射和云量），与物理全球模型相当；3) 超越传统分析变量，学习预测卫星降水估计、热带气旋及站点观测；4) 对稀疏站点数据进行建模，实现任意位置和时间的2m温度和露点预测，并利用局部地理特征进行条件约束。

Result: 模型在概率中程预报技能上达到新的SOTA；在2m温度与露点预测上，即使面对未见过的站点评估，误差也显著低于同类全球模型。

Conclusion: WeatherNext 3将基于AI的天气预测从传统"数据同化-预报-后处理"各阶段分离的仿真范式，推向统一集成的操作范式，进一步拓展了全球天气预测的性能边界与粒度精度。

Abstract: State-of-the-art AI weather models have shown impressive medium-range forecast skill and computational efficiency, but suffer two key shortcomings: their forecasts have lower spatial and temporal resolution than the best physics-based models and they are exclusively initialized with and trained on analysis data. As a result, they cannot directly make use of observations, and any biases in the analysis are inherited by the forecast. WeatherNext 3 addresses these shortcomings and establishes a new state-of-the-art for probabilistic medium-range forecasting skill. First, WeatherNext 3 generates new forecasts every hour (rather than every 6 hours like traditional global models) by ingesting low-latency geostationary satellite data. Second, WeatherNext 3's temporal and spatial resolution are on par with physics-based global models, with hourly time steps and 0.1 degree resolution for single-level variables, including solar radiation and cloud cover. Third, WeatherNext 3 moves beyond traditional analysis variables by learning to predict satellite-derived precipitation estimates, as well as tropical cyclone and station observations. Modelling sparse station data allows WeatherNext 3 to make 2m temperature and dewpoint predictions at any location and time, conditioned on local geographical features, with substantially lower error than competing global models, even when evaluated against unseen stations. Together, WeatherNext 3's capabilities move operational AI-based weather forecasting beyond emulating the traditionally distinct stages of data assimilation, forecasting and post-processing, which helps to further push the frontier of performance and granularity for global weather prediction.

</details>


### [230] [Neural-Network Maxent: a general extension with learned nonlinearity, applied to time-series for Desert Locust distribution modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03603)
*Alessandro Grassi,Edoardo Kimani Bellotto,Wassim El Azami,Sabrina Outmani,Maximilien Houel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RNN Maxent，将Maxent的固定特征字典替换为GRU神经网络，以捕捉时间序列协变量中的非线性关系，用于沙漠蝗虫栖息地适宜性建模。相比标准Maxent，在ROC AUC和F1指标上均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Maxent依赖手工选择特征的线性组合，无法捕捉生态监测中协变量（如降水、土壤湿度、植被指数）随时间演变的非线性时序关系。传统方法将时间序列展平为独立特征，丢失了关键的时间结构信息。

Method: 引入RNN Maxent：将Maxent框架中的固定特征字典替换为门控循环单元（GRU）神经网络，通过反向传播端到端训练。保留Maxent的仅有存在数据统计基础、背景归一化和概率校准，仅将固定非线性替换为从数据学习的非线性。应用50天环境时间序列（ERA5 Land、MODIS、Sentinel-3数据），协变量与存在记录间保持7天间隔以实现预测行为。

Result: 相比标准Maxent，RNN Maxent在沙漠蝗虫栖息地制图中性能提升：ROC AUC从0.792提升至0.862（std 0.036），F1从0.590提升至0.671（std 0.056）。

Conclusion: RNN Maxent通过将时序结构显式建模到Maxent框架中，显著改善了破坏性害虫如沙漠蝗虫的栖息地适宜性预测，为生态时间序列数据的物种分布建模提供了更有效的方法。

Abstract: Species Distribution Modelling (SDM) is essential for understanding how environmental conditions shape biodiversity, particularly for destructive pests such as the Desert Locust (Schistocerca gregaria), whose breeding dynamics are tightly coupled to rapidly evolving environmental conditions. Maxent has become the dominant method for presence-only data, but its reliance on a linear combination of hand chosen feature transforms limits its ability to capture the nonlinear, temporal relationships common in ecological monitoring, where covariates such as precipitation, soil moisture, and vegetation indices evolve meaningfully over time. Standard implementations flatten time-series covariates into independent features, discarding sequential structure that carries critical signal. We introduce RNN Maxent, an extension of the Maxent framework that replaces the fixed feature dictionary with a neural network, specifically a Gated Recurrent Unit (GRU), trained end to end via backpropagation. The approach preserves Maxent's presence only statistical foundations, background normalization, and probability calibration, differing only in that the nonlinearity is learned from data rather than fixed in advance. We apply RNN Maxent to map suitable habitat for the Desert Locust using 50 day environmental time series derived from ERA5 Land, MODIS, and Sentinel 3, maintaining a 7 day gap between covariates and presence records to yield forecasting behavior. Compared against standard Maxent, RNN Maxent improves performance across metrics (ROC AUC 0.862 std 0.036 vs. 0.792; F1 0.671 std 0.056 vs. 0.590).

</details>


### [231] [On the Interaction Between Model Compression and Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03604)
*Francesco Corti,Dong Wang,Young D. Kwon,Cecilia Mascolo,Olga Saukh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了结构化压缩如何影响模型在分布偏移下的测试时自适应（TTA）能力。研究发现压缩模型虽然在监督自适应下保持高精度，但其TTA性能随压缩程度增加而显著下降，根源在于表征多样性降低和结构约束限制了可恢复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在实际部署中需要同时具备高效性和适应性，即需要模型压缩和测试时自适应（TTA）。虽然二者已被广泛独立研究，但它们之间的相互作用尚未被充分理解。

Method: 使用ResNet-18和ViT-Base在CIFAR-10-C和ImageNet-C上，将多种压缩方法与标准TTA技术结合进行系统性评估；引入一个诊断框架，分别考察表征表达能力和自适应子空间兼容性。

Result: 发现一个一致的差距：压缩模型在监督自适应下保持高精度，但TTA性能随压缩加剧而显著退化。这种退化源于表征多样性降低以及结构约束限制了可恢复性，且这些效应高度依赖于压缩方法的选择。

Conclusion: 压缩会损害模型的TTA能力，压缩方法的选择对适应性有重要影响，因此需要设计能够保留模型适应性的压缩策略。

Abstract: Deep neural networks deployed in the wild must be both efficient and adaptable, requiring model compression and test-time adaptation (TTA). While both are well studied in isolation, their interaction remains poorly understood. We systematically analyze how structured compression affects a model's ability to adapt under distribution shift. Using ResNet-18 and ViT-Base on CIFAR-10-C and ImageNet-C, we evaluate multiple compression methods combined with standard TTA techniques. We introduce a diagnostic framework that examines representational expressivity and adaptation subspace compatibility. Our results reveal a consistent gap: although compressed models retain high accuracy under supervised adaptation, their TTA performance degrades significantly with increasing compression. We show that this stems from reduced representational diversity and structural constraints that limit recoverability. These effects strongly depend on the compression method, highlighting the need to design compression strategies that preserve adaptability.

</details>


### [232] [Local Updates, Global Learning (LUGL): Playing Games with non-incremental Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03660)
*David Milec,Spyridon Samothrakis,Michael Fairbank,Dennis J. N. J. Soemers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LUGL框架，将数据收集与模型拟合解耦，使梯度提升树（如LightGBM）等非增量学习器能够应用于强化学习/自博弈场景。通过在9种完美与不完美信息游戏中测试，基于LightGBM的智能体性能与DQN和DeepCFR相当或更优，挑战了社区对神经网络在博弈场景中的主导地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络在强化学习中的优势部分在于其增量学习能力，适合自博弈训练在线、非平稳的特性。然而，梯度提升树在表格型数据的监督学习中通常优于神经网络。博弈状态本质上具有表格型特征（离散动作、分类牌面、结构化棋盘位置），是树模型理想的应用场景，但非增量学习器因分布漂移问题难以直接用于RL。

Method: LUGL框架将数据收集与模型拟合解耦，交替进行两个阶段：1) 局部更新阶段（local updates）：智能体进行自博弈并在有限表格中累积表格型更新（Q值、V值、策略或后悔值）；2) 全局学习阶段（global learning）：利用表格训练一个函数逼近器以泛化到未见状态，随后重置表格。该框架使GBT等非增量学习方法能在原本会因分布漂移而失败的RL场景中运行。

Result: 在四种完美信息博弈（井字棋、四子棋、奥赛罗、六角棋）和五种不完美信息博弈（Kuhn扑克、Leduc德州扑克、吹牛骰子、Goofspiel、Flop5德州扑克）中进行测试，LUGL+LightGBM智能体的表现与DQN和DeepCFR相当或更优，在所有测试基准上均取得竞争性或优越性性能。

Conclusion: 实验结果挑战了社区对神经网络在博弈智能中的强烈偏见——基于LightGBM的智能体在所有测试基准上都能达到与NN方法竞争或更优的性能，表明表格型数据场景中树模型的价值被严重低估。

Abstract: The dominance of Neural Networks (NNs) in RL is partially due to their incremental learning capability, which naturally suits the online, non-stationary nature of self-play training. However, gradient-boosted trees like LightGBM are widely recognised as the state of the art for tabular data in supervised learning, often outperforming NNs in accuracy and efficiency. Game states are inherently tabular---discrete actions, categorical card identities, structured board positions---which makes them an ideal candidate for tree-based methods. We introduce LUGL (Local Updates, Global Learning), a framework that decouples data collection from model fitting, enabling non-incremental learners such as GBTs to operate in RL settings where they would otherwise fail due to distributional shift. LUGL alternates between a local updates phase, where the agent plays self-play games and accumulates tabular updates (Q-values, V-values, policies, or regret values) in a finite table, and a global learning phase, where the table is used to train a function approximator that generalises to unseen states before the table is reset. We test our approach in four standard perfect-information games (Tic-tac-toe, Connect-4, Othello, and Hex) and five imperfect-information games (Kuhn's poker, Leduc Hold'em, Liar's Dice, Goofspiel, and Flop5 Hold'em), and show that our results are competitive with or superior to DQN and DeepCFR. Our experiments demonstrate that the community's strong bias towards NNs in game-playing may be unwarranted, since LightGBM-based agents achieve competitive or superior performance across all tested benchmarks.

</details>


### [233] [Extracting Forgotten Prompts from Targeted Unlearned Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03662)
*Au Ashley Hoi-Ting,Meghdad Kurmanji,William F. Shen,Nicholas D. Lane,Ligang He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型攻击方法TAS（Targeted Active Search），无需预先知道被遗忘的提示词，仅通过保留数据和黑盒访问即可提取被遗忘的实体并重构被遗忘的提示，在三个数据集和三个LLM上的实验显示能100%准确恢复被遗忘实体并重构高达95%的被遗忘提示，同时查询次数比朴素探测减少99.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘研究主要关注现有攻击假设攻击者已知被遗忘的提示词并恢复其答案，但作者发现了新的漏洞：被遗忘的提示本身可以被提取。这揭示了现有遗忘方法（如NPO、DPO、LUNAR）存在的更根本的安全问题。

Method: TAS攻击分两步：(1) 通过构建规范模板和实体池，在有限查询预算下使用最具信息量的模板-实体配对来主动选择查询模型，识别被遗忘的实体；(2) 一旦实体被识别，用这些实体实例化提示模板来探测未学习模型并重构被遗忘的提示。

Result: 在三种遗忘方法、三个数据集和三个LLM上，TAS能以100%准确率恢复被遗忘实体，重构高达95%的被遗忘提示，且比朴素探测少使用99.7%的查询。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了机器遗忘方法的新漏洞——被遗忘的提示本身可以被攻击者通过主动查询从黑盒模型中提取，这对当前基于拒绝对齐的遗忘方法的有效性提出了严峻挑战，表明需要更鲁棒的遗忘机制。

Abstract: Recent unlearning methods (e.g. NPO, DPO, LUNAR) make use of refusal alignment to suppress forgotten data. However, it has been shown that refusal responses might leave traces of unlearning, and recent attacks have been able to successfully recover some of the unlearned knowledge. In this paper, we uncover a new vulnerability. Existing attacks typically assume that the forgotten prompts are already known to the adversary and focus on recovering their answers. However, we show that the forgotten prompts themselves can be extracted by using the retained data and black-box access to the model. Our attack, Targeted Active Search (TAS), first identifies the forgotten entities by constructing canonical templates and entity pool, and selectively querying the model using the most informative template-entity pair under a limited query budget. Once the entities are identified, TAS instantiates prompt templates with those entities to probe the unlearned model and reconstruct the forgotten prompts. Experiments across three unlearning methods with three datasets and three LLMs shows that TAS recovers the forgotten entity with $100\%$ accuracy and reconstructs up to $95\%$ of forgotten prompts, all while using up to $99.7\%$ fewer queries than naive probing.

</details>


### [234] [Out-of-Distribution Generalisation with Sequence Models in Offline Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03667)
*Oussama Hidaoui,Omer Ebead,Ulrich Armel Mbou Sob,Siddarth Singh,Juan Claude Formanek,Felix Chalumeau,Omayma Mahjoub,Sasha Abramowitz,Ruan John de Kock,Wiem Khlifi,Louay Ben Nessir,Simon Verster Du Toit,Daniel Rajaonarivonivelomanantsoa,Asim Awad Osman,Arnol Manuel Fokam,Refiloe Shabe,Arnu Pretorius*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统地分析了离线多智能体强化学习（MARL）中的零样本任务泛化问题，通过扩展离线序列建模架构来处理多任务观察/动作空间及可变智能体数量，发现任务多样性（而非数据集规模）是提升零样本迁移能力的决定性因素，并在四个环境中平均取得3.2倍的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线MARL中，智能体对未见任务的泛化能力仍然是一个根本性挑战。现有研究缺乏对离线环境下零样本任务泛化的原则性分析和规模化行为（任务多样性、数据集大小、网络容量）的实证探索。

Method: 扩展离线序列建模架构以处理多任务观察和动作空间，同时支持各任务中不同的智能体数量；通过大规模实验系统研究任务多样性、数据集规模与网络容量三个维度的缩放规律。

Result: 在Connector、RWARE、SMAX、LBF四个挑战性环境中，多任务方法在保留测试任务上相比单任务模型平均提升3.2倍，并始终优于强行为克隆基线。主要发现是任务多样性比单纯的数据集规模更能决定零样本迁移的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 开发可泛化的MARL智能体应优先考虑训练分布的任务多样性（尤其是包含不同智能体数量的任务），这为有效扩展离线MARL提供了路线图。

Abstract: Generalising to unseen tasks remains a fundamental challenge in offline multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL). In this work, we present a principled analysis of zero-shot task generalisation in the offline setting and conduct an extensive empirical investigation into the scaling behaviour governing task diversity, dataset size, and network capacity. To facilitate this study, we extend offline sequence modelling architectures to handle multi-task observation and action spaces alongside variable agent counts across tasks. Our primary finding is that scaling task diversity---rather than sheer dataset size is the dominant factor in achieving robust zero-shot transfer. Through large-scale experiments across four challenging environments (Connector, RWARE, SMAX, and LBF), we demonstrate that our multi-task approach achieves a mean improvement of 3.2x on held-out test tasks compared to single-task models and consistently outperforms strong behaviour cloning baselines. These results suggest that the development of generalisable MARL agents should prioritise the diversity of the training distribution with varying numbers of agents, providing a roadmap for scaling offline MARL effectively.

</details>


### [235] [Resolution-Aware Experimental Design under Partial Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03686)
*Sofianos Panagiotis Fotias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分辨率感知实验设计（RAED），在部分可识别性下选择能产生最小期望非空结构候选集且控制误排除风险的实验，证明了其与全潜变量信息增益等方法存在交叉混叠分离，并通过学习式分数实现和多项基准验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统实验设计以期望信息最大化为准则，但在部分可识别性下，持续的干扰项不确定性会使相同观测承载不同结构含义，导致基于信息增益或分类的设计在结构分辨率上可能表现任意差，需要新的准则框架。

Method: 提出RAED准则：选择在误排除控制约束下产生最小期望非空结构候选集的实验。证明了精确的交叉干扰混叠分离定理；针对复合Blackwell比较保持期望排序；开发了基于学习分数的实现，具备有限样本干扰平均和正尾校准，并刻画罕见尾样本复杂度障碍。

Result: 在受限感知下，两个地下水流基准显示RAED与EIG实验选择存在真实分歧，WCA中分辨率差异最显著；河流基准中尾保护改变了所选物理实验，将硬区域误排除主要转化为显式歧义；甲烷氧化基准中5%误排除容差对尾敏感干扰风险给出非平凡有限样本总体保证，三个结构族联合置信度95%。

Conclusion: RAED提供了一种在部分可识别性下优先保证结构分辨率的实验设计范式，理论分离结果揭示了传统信息增益准则的局限性，实证结果验证了RAED在实际受限感知问题中的优势与可操作性。

Abstract: Experimental design is commonly framed as choosing the experiment expected to provide the most information. Under partial identifiability however, persistent nuisance uncertainty can make the same observation carry different structural meanings. We introduce Resolution-Aware Experimental Design (RAED), which selects an experiment by the smallest expected nonempty structural candidate set achievable subject to false-exclusion control. We prove an exact cross-nuisance aliasing separation: an experiment can be preferred by structural and full-latent information gain, average classification, and nuisance-marginalized informativeness while having arbitrarily poorer valid structural resolution. RAED nevertheless preserves the expected ordering under a genuine composite Blackwell comparison. To make this criterion operational, we develop a learned score-based implementation with finite-sample nuisance-average and positive-tail calibration, and characterize a rare-tail sample-complexity obstruction. Under constrained sensing, two subsurface-flow benchmarks exhibit genuine RAED--expected-information-gain (EIG) experiment-selection disagreements, with the clearest and largest held-out resolution differences in WCA. In a fluvial benchmark, tail protection changes the selected physical experiment and replaces hard-region false exclusions primarily with explicit ambiguity. In a mechanistic methane-oxidation benchmark, a prospectively specified 5\% false-exclusion tolerance also yields a nontrivial finite-sample population guarantee for tail-sensitive nuisance risk, with 95\% joint confidence across all three structural families.

</details>


### [236] [Federated Causal Discovery via Regression-Directed Cumulants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03705)
*Pablo Torrijos,Fabio Stella,José A. Gámez,José M. Puerta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了联邦环境下的线性非高斯无环模型（LiNGAM），提出了FedRCD家族因果发现算法，解决了现有FedISHC方法在近对称噪声下失效的问题，并通过高阶累积量张量实现单轮通信。实验发现累积量类联邦方法在典型样本量下并非按总体不对称性排序变量，而是按DAG有向路径诱导的方差阶梯排序，边际标准化会使方法退化为随机排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多实际领域数据稀缺，且因GDPR等法规不宜集中各客户端数据。联邦学习提供了平衡隐私与因果发现精度的方案，但标准集中式DirectLiNGAM不能直接联邦化。高阶累积量张量可实现单轮通信，但现有FedISHC在近对称噪声下失效，需要新方法克服这一局限。

Method: 提出FedRCD因果发现算法家族，包含三个变体，在通信轮次与代数噪声之间权衡。其中两个是集中式高阶累积量（HC）和HC-LiNGAM算法的精确联邦对应版本，单轮变体支持从单条观测到整个客户端的任意粒度精确去学习，适用于水平、垂直和混合数据划分。

Result: 数值实验表明，在真实部署的典型样本量下，整个累积量联邦家族并非按分数编码的总体不对称性排序变量，而是按DAG有向路径诱导的方差阶梯排序（即累积量版的varsortability）。边际标准化会使所有累积量方法退化为近似随机排序，而尺度不变的DirectLiNGAM不受影响（但该协议下不可联邦化）。

Conclusion: FedRCD家族提供了联邦环境下超越马尔可夫等价的因果发现方案，克服了FedISHC在近对称噪声下的局限，支持精确去学习。但研究发现累积量方法在有限样本下实际排序由方差阶梯主导而非不对称性，揭示了这类方法在联邦实际部署中的内在局限。

Abstract: In this paper we study linear non-Gaussian acyclic models (LiNGAM) when used in federated environments. These causal models allow one to go beyond Markov equivalence. However, in many domains data are scarce, and increasing the sample size by centralising data from different clients is not advisable due to regulations such as the GDPR. The federated environment offers an attractive option to balance privacy and causal discovery accuracy. Unfortunately, the standard centralised estimator in the LiNGAM setting, i.e., DirectLiNGAM, cannot be straightforwardly federated. Higher-order cumulant tensors offer a way around this obstacle: they depend only on the joint distribution of the variables involved and add exactly across independent sample groups, so a single communication round suffices in horizontal, vertical, and hybrid partitions.
  However, FedISHC, i.e., the current federated method along these lines, breaks down under near-symmetric noise. To overcome the above limitation, we introduce the FedRCD family of causal discovery algorithms, and investigate three variants that trade off communication rounds against algebraic noise; two of them are exact federated counterparts of the centralised high-order cumulant (HC) and HC-LiNGAM algorithms, and the single-round variants further effectively support exact unlearning at any granularity, from a single observation to a whole client. Numerical experiments show that at sample sizes typical of real deployments, the entire cumulant-based federated family does not actually rank variables by the population asymmetry that the scores encode at zero. It ranks them by a variance ladder induced by the DAG along its directed paths, the cumulant counterpart of varsortability. Marginal standardisation collapses every cumulant method to near-random ordering, while scale-invariant DirectLiNGAM, not federable under this protocol, is unaffected.

</details>


### [237] [Projected Riemannian Gradient Descent for the Bures-Wasserstein Barycenter: Dimension-Independent Linear Convergence at Unit Step Size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03762)
*A. Afham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了Bures-Wasserstein重心计算的收敛性分析困境：单位步长Riemannian梯度下降(RGD)收敛快但缺乏维度无关的理论保证，而小步长保证维度无关却牺牲了实际速度。作者提出Projected RGD算法，通过新颖的投影引理（将正定矩阵特征值裁剪到区间[α,β]是闭式、非扩张的BW度量投影），实现单位步长下的维度无关线性收敛，速率为(1-κ^{-3/2})，并将相同分析推广到Brahmachari等人的不变矩阵投影问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分析存在二分法困境：单位步长RGD的保证在最坏情况下随维度指数增长，而维度无关的保证需要小步长从而丧失实际中观察到的快速收敛。作者希望打破这一困境，同时获得维度无关性和单位步长的快速收敛。

Method: 提出Projected RGD算法：在每次迭代中将当前矩阵的特征值裁剪到区间[α,β]内。关键贡献是证明了一个新型投影引理——特征值裁剪操作是BW度量下到集合{S:αI≤S≤βI}的闭式非扩张(1-Lipschitz)投影，这与已知的单侧版本不同，不能仅由凸性推出。该投影是"免费"的，因为它复用了下一次迭代必须执行的特征分解。此外，作者将Brahmachari等人的不动点算法识别为完全测地子流形上的单位步长RGD，从而将维度无关保证直接推广。

Result: Projected RGD在单位步长下实现了维度无关的线性收敛，速率为(1-κ^{-3/2})，其中κ是集合的条件数。迭代复杂度为κ^{3/2}，多项式地优于小步长保证的κ^{5/2}迭代复杂度。相同的分析以逐字不变的方式推广到Brahmachari等人的不变矩阵投影问题，并且投影步骤不增加每步的计算成本。

Conclusion: 通过引入特征值裁剪的投影技术，作者成功弥合了理论保证与实际性能之间的差距，同时获得维度无关性和单位步长的最优收敛速率，并扩展了该方法的适用范围。

Abstract: The computation of the Bures-Wasserstein (BW) barycenter of an ensemble of positive definite matrices arises throughout machine learning, optimal transport, and quantum information. Riemannian gradient descent (RGD) at unit step size -- the fixed-point iteration used in practice -- converges rapidly, yet existing analyses present a dichotomy: unit-step guarantees carry worst-case exponential dependence on the dimension, while dimension-independent guarantees require small step sizes that forfeit the empirical speed. We resolve this dichotomy, not by improving the guarantees for unit-step RGD, but by proposing a Projected RGD algorithm that achieves dimension-independent linear convergence at unit step size. The achieved rate, $(1 - κ^{-3/2})$, where $κ$ is the condition number of the ensemble, also polynomially improves on the best small-step guarantee ($κ^{3/2}$ versus $κ^{5/2}$ iteration complexity). The crux is a novel Projection Lemma: clipping the eigenvalues of a positive matrix to an interval $[α, β]$ is the closed-form, non-expansive (1-Lipschitz) BW-metric projection onto the set $\{S : αI \leq S \leq βI\}$ -- a statement which, unlike its known one-sided counterpart, does not follow from convexity. The projection is moreover free: it reuses an eigendecomposition the next iteration must perform in any case, so the projected and unprojected iterations cost the same per step. The same analysis covers the invariant matrix projection problem of Brahmachari et al. (2025), whose fixed-point algorithm we identify as unit-step RGD on a totally geodesic submanifold, thereby extending the dimension-independent guarantee to that setting verbatim.

</details>


### [238] [From Nowcasting to Forecasting: Adapting a Reanalysis-Trained](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03763)
*Mikko Partio,Leila Hieta,Ossi Laine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CloudCast v2，一种基于观测初始条件的12小时云量预报机器学习模型。该模型先在Copernicus欧洲区域再分析数据上训练学习云演化动力学，再通过条件流匹配方法适应卫星观测云场。相比前代CloudCast v1，在1-12小时范围内平均绝对误差降低10%，且约3-6小时后在空间一致性的分数技能评分上超越前代，证明观测初始化的机器学习预报可超越传统1-3小时临近预报范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的云量预报对温度预测、辐射预报和太阳能发电运行至关重要。短时临近预报在前几小时能保留观测云位置但技能迅速下降；而更长预报时效需要数值天气预报，但初始场可能无法准确表示卫星观测的云状态。因此需要一种既能从观测初始条件出发，又能学习云场演化动力学的模型。

Method: CloudCast v2采用两阶段训练策略：首先在Copernicus欧洲区域再分析数据上训练以学习云演化动态，然后使用条件流匹配这一生成式方法，将噪声变换为以观测初始云场和NWP输入为条件的云量预报，从而适配卫星观测云场。

Result: CloudCast v2在1-12小时范围内相对前代CloudCast v1将平均绝对误差降低10%；在分数技能评分（衡量空间一致性的邻域指标）上，根据云量类别不同，约在3-6小时后超越CloudCast v1。

Conclusion: 观测初始化的机器学习预报能够保留卫星云场的空间细节，同时将预报时效扩展到通常的1-3小时临近预报范围之外，实现12小时的有效云量预报。

Abstract: Accurate cloud-cover forecasts are important for temperature prediction, radiation forecasting, and solar-power operations. Short-range forecasting methods can preserve observed cloud placement during the first forecast hours, but their skill decreases when cloud fields evolve through formation, dissipation and deformation. Longer lead times require accounting for atmospheric evolution, but operational numerical weather prediction (NWP) forecasts may not accurately represent the satellite-observed cloud state at initialization. We develop CloudCast v2, a machine-learning model for 12-hour cloud-cover forecasting from observation-based initial conditions. The model is first trained on the Copernicus European Regional Reanalysis (Ridal2024) to learn cloud-evolution dynamics, and is then adapted to satellite-derived cloud fields using conditional flow matching (Lipman2023), a generative method that transforms noise into cloud-cover forecasts conditioned on the observed initial cloud fields and NWP inputs. CloudCast v2 reduces mean absolute error by 10% relative to its predecessor, CloudCast v1 (Partio2025), over the 1-12 h range. It also overtakes CloudCast v1 in fractions skill score, a neighborhood-based measure of spatial agreement, after approximately 3-6 h, depending on the cloudiness category. These results show that observation-initialized machine-learning forecasts can extend beyond the usual 1-3-hour nowcasting range while retaining spatial detail from satellite cloud fields.

</details>


### [239] [OBER+: Continuity-Aware Reporting and Traceable Continuous Improvement in Outcome-Based Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03770)
*Elakkiya Rajasekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OBER+，一个扩展的成果达成度平台，它完成了从测量到的差距到评估后纠正措施的自动计算。通过五个连续阶段（累积达成度、标记差距和持续性差距、按监管阈值分级、对照带证据的实践目录记录决策、记录变更、量化差距后续变化），以及一个比较成果声明前后版本的规则，确保达成度数据不会跨越成果变更点被错误读成连续序列。在两个真实课程上的应用发现：核心课程的所有成果在连续交付间都被实质性重新定义；文档化规则重算暴露了平台缺陷；超过半数成果编号在变更后指向了不同编号的先前成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然许多机构常规计算基于成果的教育（OBE）的学习成果达成度，但课程分析综述报告显示缺乏证据说明这些计算结果如何实际指导决策。现有平台止步于"测量差值"，没有完成从差距到纠正行动的自动化闭环。

Method: OBER+含五个连接阶段：跨课程多次交付累积达成度、发出差距及持续性差距信号、按监管机构现有阈值对差距分级、对照带证据标注的实践目录记录决定、记录变更并量化差距的后续移动。另设规则比较成果声明的先后版本，防止成果变更点被误读为连续序列。

Result: 在两条真实课程上应用该规则得出三项结果：(1) 核心课程的每个成果在连续交付间都被实质性重新定义，内容在不同成果编号间移动，若朴素解读会报告25个百分点的错误下跌；(2) 按文档化规则重算平台数字，十项中有六项差异超出舍入可解释范围，模式指向已上报机构的缺陷；(3) 在三个转变期的15对声明中，5对逐字相同，10对不同者中有6个成果编号所承载的内容最接近编号不同的先前成果。

Conclusion: 本文贡献在于为基于成果的报告提供了一个可计算的设计，以任何达成度平台都能实现的规则形式陈述，并用真实机构记录中的证据展示了这些规则所能揭示的问题。

Abstract: Institutions practising outcome-based education compute learning outcome attainment routinely, while reviews of curriculum analytics report an absence of evidence on how that computation informs decisions. This paper presents OBER+, an extension of a deployed institutional attainment platform that computes the step from a measured shortfall to an evaluated corrective action. Five connected stages accumulate attainment across deliveries of a course, signal a shortfall and a persistent shortfall, grade it on cutoffs the regulator already uses, record the decision against a catalogue of practices annotated with their evidence, log the change, and quantify the subsequent movement in the shortfall. A further rule compares successive statements of an outcome, so attainment is never read as a series across a point at which the outcome changed. Applying the rules to the live record of two real courses produced three results. Every outcome of a core course was substantively redefined between consecutive deliveries, with subject matter moving between outcome numbers, so a naive reading would have reported a twenty-five point collapse between quantities that do not refer to the same learning. Recomputing the platform's figures from its documented rule showed six of ten differing by more than rounding explains, in a pattern that identified a defect since reported to the institution. Across fifteen statement pairs from three transitions, five were identical character for character, and among the ten that were not, the outcome carrying a given number was nearest to a differently numbered earlier outcome in six, a result resting on an ordering of similarities and requiring no threshold and no labelling. The contribution is a computational design for outcome-based reporting, stated as rules any attainment platform can implement, with evidence of what they make visible in a live institutional record.

</details>


### [240] [Landmark-Based Discrimination of Injury-Associated Athlete-Sessions from Minute-Resolution Multimodal Football Monitoring Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03790)
*Evangelos Chatzidimitriou,Konstantinos Tserpes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"固定里程碑、每位运动员-会话一个表示"的新颖建模框架，用于运动员监测数据中的损伤预测。该方法解决了分钟级监测数据与会话级损伤标签之间的粒度不匹配问题，在2020 SoccerMon数据集上评估了多种表示方法，结果显示模型辨识能力有限且不确定性较大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动员监测数据通常是分钟级的（每场比赛或训练每分钟都有记录），但损伤信息只能精确到会话级别（整场会话是否相关）。直接将会话级标签赋予每一分钟会导致建模问题：它暗示了损伤状态在每一时刻都是已知的，而实际上会话内的损伤发生时间未知。这种数据粒度不匹配是现有建模方法的核心痛点。

Method: 核心创新是固定里程碑（fixed-landmark）、每位运动员-会话一个表示（one-representation-per-athlete-session）的公式化方法。在每个里程碑（如10、20、30分钟）构建该运动员-会话的一个表示，仅使用截至该时间点观察到的信息，保持目标为会话级而非分钟级，避免无依据的分钟级损伤监督。评估了会话前（pre-session）、累积（cumulative）、动态（dynamic）和组合表示，并使用了运动员不相交验证、运动员聚类bootstrap不确定性、共同队列敏感性分析等严格验证方法。

Result: 在2020 SoccerMon数据上分析了3743个运动员-会话（来自48名精英女子足球运动员，包含5名运动员的22个损伤相关会话）。主要CUM+DYN逻辑回归模型在各里程碑上ROC-AUC为0.367-0.607，PR-AUC为0.0080-0.0150，置信区间很宽。包含PRE（会话前）信息的表示在某些里程碑上点估计更高，但同样不确定。整体辨识能力有限。

Conclusion: 该研究系统化地解决了运动员监测中时间粒度错配的方法论问题，但其实证结果（低AUC、极低的PR-AUC和宽不确定性）表明当前数据量（仅22个损伤会话）和方法框架下的损伤预测挑战极大，需要更多数据和更精细的方法来获得可靠的预测能力。

Abstract: Athlete monitoring data may be recorded minute by minute throughout a match or training session, while injury information may only indicate whether the entire session was injury-associated.
  This creates a modelling problem: assigning the same session-level label to every minute would imply that injury status is known at each exact time, even though within-session injury onset is unknown.
  Our novelty is a fixed-landmark, one-representation-per-athlete-session formulation that directly addresses this mismatch. Instead of labelling every minute, we construct one representation per athlete-session at each landmark using information observed up to that point. This keeps the target at the session level and avoids unsupported minute-level injury supervision.
  A landmark is a fixed time point within the same session, such as 10, 20, or 30 minutes. At each landmark, we assess whether the whole session is injury-associated or non-injury-associated and examine how discrimination changes as more within-session information becomes available.
  Using 2020 SoccerMon data, we analyse 3,743 athlete-sessions from 48 elite women's football athletes, including 22 injury-associated sessions from five athletes. We evaluate pre-session, cumulative, dynamic, and combined representations with athlete-disjoint validation, athlete-cluster bootstrap uncertainty, common-cohort sensitivity analysis, alternative negative-athlete fold allocations, equal-athlete weighting, and Logistic Regression, Random Forest, and XGBoost benchmarks.
  Primary CUM+DYN Logistic Regression yields ROC-AUC 0.367-0.607 and PR-AUC 0.0080-0.0150 across landmarks, with wide uncertainty. PRE-containing representations show higher point estimates at several landmarks but remain uncertain.

</details>


### [241] [From Ordered Bernoulli Levels to Critical-Line Geometry: Integer Quantization, Bernoulli Residual Phase, and Prime-Power Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03801)
*Y. Kenan Yılmaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了有序伯努利词核 f(p,n,k)=p^k(1-p)^(n-k)，及其倒数整数水平集的几何结构。通过复连续性延拓，在引入任何zeta函数之前构造了共轭对称的垂直几何。对临界线零点纵坐标 gamma_k，其诱导水平 L_k=1/4+gamma_k^2 可分解为最近整数 N_k 与周期一阶伯努利残差 delta_k 之和。通过圆化提取相位变量，并借助唯一分解与复指数 s 将构造提升至 Dirichlet 原子与 Euler 乘积结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图建立一种不依赖 zeta 函数先验输入的独立几何框架，用以描述 Riemann 临界线零点与伯努利词核之间的深层结构关联，从而为理解临界线零点分布提供新的组合-解析视角。

Method: 从伯努利词核出发，取二分水平集 2^(-n)，选定 p=1/2 作为唯一实数锚点；经补集保持的复延拓得到 z=1/2±iu 的共轭对称垂直几何；引入二次坐标 Q(z)=1/4+u^2 并给出精确整数量化；将临界线零点诱导水平分解为整数部分+伯努利周期残差；通过圆化 Z_k=exp(2πiδ_k) 提取 gamma_k^2 mod 1 相位；利用唯一因子分解和复指数 s 连接 Dirichlet 级数与 Euler 乘积。

Result: 得到了精确的整数分解恒等式，建立了残差相位变量与临界零点坐标的对应关系，并将构造推广至 Dirichlet 原子层。经典 zeta 连接与数值验证被明确区分，条件性 Weyl 检验保持开放。

Conclusion: 论文构建了一个独立于 zeta 函数的伯努利几何框架，提供了临界线零点的精确整数分解与相位表示，但明确不声称证明 Riemann 假设，所有条件性结论均需额外检验。

Abstract: We study the ordered Bernoulli-word kernel f(p,n,k)=p^k(1-p)^(n-k) and the geometry generated by its inverse-integer level sets. The binary level 2^(-n) selects p=1/2 as the unique real split-independent anchor. Under complement-preserving complex continuation, the pair becomes z=1/2+iu and 1-z=1/2-iu, producing a conjugation-symmetric vertical geometry before any zeta-function input is introduced. The quadratic coordinate Q(z)=z(1-z)=1/4+u^2 has a sharp minimum at the central point and admits an exact integer quantization. For critical-line zero ordinates gamma_k, the induced levels L_k=1/4+gamma_k^2 are decomposed exactly as L_k=N_k+delta_k, where N_k is the nearest integer and delta_k is a periodic first-Bernoulli residual. Circularization gives Z_k=exp(2 pi i delta_k), isolating gamma_k^2 mod 1 as the residual phase variable. Unique factorization resolves the integer shells into prime-generator coordinates, while a distinct complex exponent s lifts the same construction to the Dirichlet atoms m^(-s), linking the Dirichlet-series and Euler-product assemblies. Exact identities, classical zeta connections, numerical controls, and open conditional Weyl tests are kept explicitly separate. No proof of the Riemann Hypothesis is claimed.

</details>


### [242] [Free Pause Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03807)
*John Langford,Nathan Godey,Giovanni Monea,Yoav Artzi,Harry Dong,Ying Fan,Gustavo de Rosa,Zheng Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"自由暂停令牌"(free pause token)机制，语言模型在每次预测下一个token时拥有额外计算资源（类似暂停/思考令牌），但与标准序列token不同，该计算以并行的预测流形式在权重共享的骨干网络上运行，而非向序列中添加额外token，从而在推理时几乎零成本地提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的暂停/思考令牌(pause/thinking token)虽然能通过额外计算提升预测质量，但会占用序列长度和KV缓存，增加推理成本。作者希望在不增加序列长度和KV缓存的前提下获得类似的好处。

Method: 在权重共享的骨干网络上引入一个并行的预测流作为"暂停令牌"，而非将其作为序列中的额外token。额外计算占据现有位置而非新增位置，因此在推理时不会增加上下文长度和KV缓存，也不会显著增加延迟；主要成本在训练阶段，相比优化后的预训练流程，训练计算量可控制在x1.14左右。

Result: 在1B参数模型上，该方法将下一个token的预测质量提升了2-3个centinats（perplexity单位）。在等计算量(isoflop)、等参数量(isoparameter)和等token数(isotoken)的条件下均优于标准next-token训练的transformer。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种"免费"的推理时计算增强方式——无需额外序列长度、KV缓存或显著延迟即可获得更好的预测性能，唯一的代价是训练阶段约x1.14的计算量增加。

Abstract: A free pause token gives a language model extra compute to form each next-token prediction (as a pause, or thinking, token does) but carries that compute in a parallel prediction stream over a weight-shared backbone rather than as an extra token in the sequence. It improves next-token prediction by 2-3 centinats in practice on a 1B parameter model. Because the pause rides an existing position instead of adding one, it is free to use: at inference it adds no context length, no KV cache, and essentially no latency with the growth in inference flops typically irrelevant as it is not the active bottleneck on throughput. The only primary cost is in training, where additional training compute versus an optimized pretraining pipeline is reduced to as low as x1.14 while preserving most of the benefits. The result is an isoflop, isoparameter, and isotoken improvement over standard next token trained transformers.

</details>


### [243] [A Peer-Relative Representation Learning Framework for Energy Inefficiency Identification in Mobile Network Sites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03809)
*Eliud Nyakweba Koto,Jaco du Toit,Adham Stoltz,Johan du Preez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无监督的同类参照（peer-relative）方法来检测移动网络中站点级别的能源低效问题。该方法扩展了最小失真嵌入（MDE）目标函数，引入基于能量的排斥机制，使能源消耗异常高的站点在嵌入空间中偏离其局部邻域，从而识别潜在的低效站点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源消耗是移动网络运营商最大的运营支出之一，但站点级能源低效（如故障冷却控制器、闲置无线设备、寄生辅助负载）通常难以检测，因为缺乏地面真值标签，且历史测量数据本身可能已包含嵌入的低效问题。

Method: 提出能量感知的MDE（Energy-aware Minimum Distortion Embedding）方法，扩展标准MDE目标函数，加入基于能量的排斥机制。该方法基于"具有相似结构和运营特征的站点应具有可比能源消耗"的前提，在低维嵌入空间中同时保留结构相似性并编码能源相关偏差，实现同类参照比较。

Result: 实验结果表明该方法优于传统异常检测基线，能为大规模移动网络能源效率优化提供稳健基础。异常分数可为现场调查优先级提供实用机制，帮助运营商将工程资源集中在最可能产生节能收益的站点上。

Conclusion: 所提出的无监督同类参照方法能够有效识别移动网络中的能源低效站点，为运营商提供了一种无需先验标签即可定位低效问题的可行方案，支持大规模能源效率优化。

Abstract: Energy consumption is one of the largest operational expenditure items for mobile network operators, yet site-level energy inefficiencies such as faulty cooling controllers, idle radio equipment, and parasitic auxiliary loads often remain undetected because no ground-truth inefficiency labels exist and historical measurements may already contain embedded inefficiencies. This study proposes an unsupervised peer-relative approach based on the premise that sites with similar structural and operational characteristics should exhibit comparable energy consumption. To capture these relationships, a novel energy-aware Minimum Distortion Embedding (MDE) formulation is introduced that extends the standard MDE objective with an energy-based repulsion mechanism. This encourages sites with anomalously high energy consumption relative to comparable peers to become displaced from their local neighbourhoods in the embedding space. The resulting low-dimensional representation simultaneously preserves structural similarity and encodes energy-related deviations, enabling the identification of potentially inefficient sites through peer-relative comparison. The derived anomaly scores provide a practical mechanism for prioritising field investigations, allowing mobile network operators to focus engineering resources on sites most likely to yield energy savings. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed approach outperforms conventional anomaly detection baselines and provides a robust foundation for large-scale energy-efficiency optimisation in mobile networks.

</details>


### [244] [Witnesses Explain Anomalies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03826)
*Lamine Diop*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WAND是一种可解释的、无监督表格异常检测器，通过将数据点投影到单位球面上的方向来打分，其"见证方向"天然构成特征级解释，无需事后SHAP/LIME。在47个ADBench数据集上达到最佳平均Friedman排名，且ROC-AUC与16个基线持平，解释准确性和忠实度更高，计算成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督异常检测器虽然能打分，但无法解释为什么某个点被标记为异常。现有主流检测器只输出分数而不说明特征归因，事后用SHAP或LIME补解释既需数千次重新查询又只是近似。需要一个"设计即解释"的检测器，让解释与打分零额外成本地自然产生。

Method: WAND围绕单位球面上的方向组织计算，将每个点投影到方向上的偏离程度与亚高斯极值基线比较来打分。关键创新在于：标记异常点的"见证方向"本身就是特征空间中的向量，因此直接构成每个特征的归因解释，无需额外计算；分数可微，也可通过梯度回溯解释。打分复杂度与样本量线性，并有探测效率界保证每个异常必有其见证方向（因此必有解释）。

Result: 在47个ADBench数据集上，WAND取得最佳平均Friedman排名，ROC-AUC与16种无监督基线持平。其原生解释比事后SHAP/LIME及ECOD更准确、更忠实，且查询成本仅为其一小部分。

Conclusion: WAND证明了解释性可以在不损失检测精度的前提下"融入设计"而非事后附加，是以实用性为导向的可解释异常检测方案，兼顾准确性、忠实度和计算效率。

Abstract: Unsupervised anomaly detection scores each point of an unlabelled, contaminated sample in a single pass, and increasingly must also explain why a point is flagged. Yet the dominant detectors give a score with no account of which features drive it, and explanations are bolted on post-hoc with SHAP or LIME, which re-query the detector thousands of times per point and only approximate it. We introduce WAND, an unsupervised tabular anomaly detector that is explainable by design. WAND organises its computation around directions on the unit sphere, scoring each point by how far its projection escapes a sub-Gaussian extreme-value baseline. The originality of our approach is that the witness directions that flag a point, being vectors in feature space, are its explanation, a per-feature attribution obtained at no cost over scoring and, since the score is differentiable, recoverable by gradients. Scoring is linear in the sample size, and a probe-efficiency bound guarantees every anomaly a witness, hence an explanation. Across 47 ADBench datasets WAND attains the best mean Friedman rank at ROC-AUC parity with 16 unsupervised baselines, so the gain is interpretability at no accuracy cost; its native explanations are more accurate and faithful than post-hoc SHAP/LIME and ECOD at a fraction of the query cost. WAND is thus a practical, interpretable solution for explainable anomaly detection.

</details>


### [245] [Multi-step Proximal Policy Improvement in Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03842)
*Soohyun Choi,Seonvin Cho,Songnam Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种离线强化学习的几何视角，将策略视为概率流形，把广泛的离线actor目标视为由critic定义能量的流形梯度流的隐式离散化（即单步近端策略改进SPI）。在此基础上提出多步近端策略改进（MPI），通过组合序列化的重新中心化近端步骤，在保持近端控制的同时实现超出数据集支持的策略改进。实验表明MPI在D4RL基准上提升了TD3+BC、ReBRAC和IQL等强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习面临两难：策略更新需要停留在数据集支持的范围内以保证价值估计可靠，但真正的性能提升往往需要超出行为分布。现有方法缺乏统一的几何视角来理解这两种竞争性需求之间的权衡。

Method: 1) 将策略建模为赋予特定度量几何的概率流形；2) 证明广泛的离线actor目标可解释为SPI（隐式离散化的流形梯度流）；3) 提出MPI，由多个重新中心化的近端步骤组成，逐步推进策略改进；4) 支持多种策略几何（确定性、对角高斯），作为即插即用的精化机制。

Result: 在D4RL基准上，少量MPI精化步骤即可在多个任务上提升TD3+BC、ReBRAC和IQL等强离线基线。消融诊断进一步区分了重新中心化精化与固定目标更新调度，并刻画了在critic误差下的局限。

Conclusion: MPI提供了一种统一且实用的框架来平衡离线RL中的近端约束与策略探索，通过多步几何流实现了可控的数据集外策略改进，并展示了其广泛的适用性与改进潜力。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) must reconcile two competing requirements: policy updates should stay near dataset-supported actions to keep value estimates reliable, yet meaningful gains often require moving beyond the behavior distribution. We develop a geometric view of offline actor updates by modeling policies as a probability manifold endowed with a chosen metric geometry. Under this lens, a broad class of offline actor objectives can be interpreted as a single proximal policy improvement step (SPI), i.e., an implicit discretization of a manifold gradient flow induced by a critic-defined energy. Building on this insight, we propose multi-step proximal policy improvement (MPI), a plug-in refinement mechanism that composes sequential re-centered proximal steps. MPI enables controlled policy improvement beyond dataset support while retaining proximal control at each refinement. The framework accommodates multiple policy geometries and admits practical instantiations for deterministic and diagonal-Gaussian policies. Experiments on D4RL benchmarks show that small numbers of MPI refinements improve strong offline baselines, including TD3+BC, ReBRAC, and IQL, on many tasks. Focused diagnostics further distinguish re-centered refinement from fixed-objective update scheduling and characterize limitations under critic error.

</details>


### [246] [Pushing the (Decision) Boundaries: Dynamically Calibrating Differentially Private Noise to Explainability in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03851)
*Michael Khavkin,Kichang Lee,Jaeho Jin,JeongGil Ko,Eran Toch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出XCal-FL，一种面向跨站点联邦学习的闭环可解释性驱动本地训练算法，通过三个互补信号（预测logit变化、反事实边界、显著性集中度）动态校准差分隐私噪声，在保证正式DP保证的同时提升模型准确性和解释保真度。在三个医学影像数据集上的实验表明，XCal-FL相较于静态噪声FL提升预测性能超过10%，解释保真度提升最高5倍，并实现更高的隐私预算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带差分隐私的联邦学习虽能保护数据隐私，但DP噪声会扭曲学习表征并降低解释保真度，这在需要可信解释的决策关键场景（如辅助临床诊断）中受限。先前工作仅用静态特征重要性信号来适配DP噪声，将可解释性局限于事后分析，无法在训练过程中根据解释质量校准噪声。

Method: XCal-FL是一个闭环的、可解释性驱动的本地训练算法，从三个互补信号动态校准DP噪声：(1)预测logit变化，度量对模型置信度的因果影响；(2)反事实边界，捕捉决策边界敏感性；(3)显著性集中度，量化模型注意力的空间连贯性。同时通过自适应隐私核算实现正式的DP保证。

Result: 在三个医学影像数据集及多种FL配置下，XCal-FL相比静态噪声FL提升预测性能超过10%，解释保真度提升最高达5倍，并在保真度上优于SOTA自适应DP方法。XCal-FL还实现更高的隐私预算效率，且揭示解释保真度与隐私损失呈非线性关系，而预测性能大致呈线性关系。

Conclusion: 可解释性是隐私权衡中的一个独特维度，无法仅凭效用指标推断。该发现对决策关键应用中的训练和隐私预算分配具有重要启示。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) with Differential Privacy (DP) is increasingly adopted to preserve data confidentiality in distributed machine learning. However, DP noise distorts learned representations and degrades explanation fidelity, limiting differentially private FL where trustworthy explanations are required, such as assistive clinical diagnosis. Prior work adapted DP noise with static feature-importance signals, restricting explainability to post hoc analysis and precluding noise calibration to explanation quality during training. We propose XCal-FL, a closed-loop, explainability-driven local training algorithm for image classification in cross-silo FL that dynamically calibrates DP noise from three complementary signals: (1) prediction logit variations, measuring causal influence on model confidence, (2) counterfactual margins, capturing decision-boundary sensitivity, and (3) saliency concentration, quantifying spatial coherence of model attention, while enforcing formal DP guarantees via adaptive privacy accounting. Experiments on three medical imaging datasets across varying FL configurations show that XCal-FL yields more accurate and interpretable global models, improving predictive performance by over 10\% and explanation fidelity by up to 5$\times$ over static-noise FL, and outperforming state-of-the-art adaptive DP methods in fidelity. XCal-FL also achieves higher privacy-budget efficiency, turning each unit of cumulative privacy loss into larger gains in both accuracy and explanation fidelity. Our analysis further reveals that, unlike predictive performance, which scales roughly linearly with privacy loss, explanation fidelity exhibits non-linear dynamics. These findings suggest explainability is a distinct dimension of the privacy trade-off that cannot be inferred from utility alone, with implications for training and privacy-budget allocation in decision-critical applications.

</details>


### [247] [High-Dimensional Learning Dynamics of Attention-Indexed Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03858)
*Yizhou Xu,Margarita Sagitova,Lenka Zdeborová,Florent Krzakala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了注意力机制的训练动力学，提出了注意力索引模型框架，揭示了注意力参数化本身可作为一种架构隐式偏置，并分析了不同参数化形式（直接、绑定、非绑定）下的学习动力学与样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力机制是现代基础模型的核心，但其训练动力学，特别是在注意力矩阵具有广泛秩时的行为仍未被充分理解。

Method: 提出了注意力索引模型这一广泛框架，可表示多层和多头注意力架构。在高维极限下用有限个迹序参数刻画群体损失景观，用无限矩阵矩层级（可被有限截断系统指数级逼近）刻画在线SGD动力学。

Result: 发现三种参数化形式的截然不同行为：直接优化注意力矩阵S可能陷入无信息状态；绑定注意力S=WW^T引入自动对称破缺机制，实现Θ(d²log d)样本的弱恢复；非绑定注意力S=UV^T呈现快慢机制——预激活均值快演化，重叠慢演化，快动力学选态破缺对称时可实现弱恢复。

Conclusion: 注意力参数化结构本身构成一种架构隐式偏置，影响优化动力学与样本效率，凸显了参数化选择在注意力机制训练中的关键作用。

Abstract: Attention mechanisms are central to modern foundation models, yet their training dynamics remain poorly understood, especially when the attention matrices have extensive rank. In this work, we study attention-indexed models, a broad framework that can represent multi-layer and multi-head attention architectures. First, we show that, in a suitable high-dimensional limit, the population-loss landscape is characterized by a finite set of trace order parameters. In contrast, online stochastic gradient descent (SGD) is governed by an infinite hierarchy of matrix moments, which we show can be exponentially well-approximated by a finite truncated system. Second, this framework reveals that attention parameterization itself can act as an architectural implicit bias. Direct optimization of an attention matrix $S\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}$ can remain trapped in an uninformative state. Tied attention ($S=WW^\top$) induces an automatic symmetry-breaking mechanism and yields weak recovery in $Θ(d^2\log d)$ samples. For untied attention, $S=UV^\top$, we uncover a fast-slow mechanism: the pre-activation mean first evolves on a fast timescale, while the overlaps evolve on a slower one. Weak recovery on the $Θ(d^2\log d)$ scale occurs when the state selected by the fast dynamics breaks the initial symmetry.

</details>


### [248] [Differentiable Interval Bottlenecks for Interpretable Anomaly Detection in Numerical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03878)
*Lamine Diop,Marc Plantevit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DIFFINT，一种可解释的基于重构的异常检测方法。它通过将自编码器的潜变量瓶颈结构化为软轴对齐区间成员（soft axis-aligned interval memberships），直接从原始数值数据端到端学习，无需离散化或二值化。每个潜单元对应特征空间中的一个可读超矩形，实例的编码强度反映其落入各区间的情况，重构误差作为异常分数。该方法在48个ADBench基准上与22个基线对比，取得最佳平均排名（ROC-AUC 4.10，AUPR 4.16），且是统计上并列领先的七个方法中唯一的可解释检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于重构的异常检测器（如深度自编码器）虽然准确但缺乏可解释性——它能标记异常样本，却无法告诉使用者哪些特征范围导致异常。作者希望保留可微表示学习的强大能力，同时暴露可检查的内部结构，让检测结果可审计。

Method: DIFFINT的核心方法包括：(1) 将自编码器瓶颈重构为一组软轴对齐区间成员，直接从原始数据端到端学习；(2) 每个潜单元对应可读超矩形，编码强度由落入区间的相对强度决定，重构误差作为异常分数；(3) 通过Lipschitz约束的解码器提供认证的重构误差下界（针对落在学习支持之外的点）；(4) 提出逐级抑制机制处理只有少数特征异常的情况；(5) 提供闭式、无需标签的重要性度量，用于对(单元, 特征)对排序，将训练好的区间转化为可审计的候选约束。

Result: 在48个ADBench基准上对比22个基线的统一[-1,1]归一化协议下，DIFFINT在两个指标上均取得最佳平均排名（ROC-AUC为4.10，AUPR为4.16）。在纯内点检测器中处于领先地位，与最强的含污染数据检测器竞争可达。它是统计上并列领先的七个方法中唯一可解释的检测器。

Conclusion: DIFFINT表明可解释性与检测性能可以兼得——通过结构化的区间潜变量设计和严格的归纳偏置（认证下界+抑制机制），它在保持顶级检测精度的同时提供了可审计的内部结构，为可解释异常检测树立了新的标杆。

Abstract: Reconstruction-based anomaly detectors are accurate but opaque: a deep autoencoder flags a sample without telling a practitioner which feature ranges made it anomalous. We propose DIFFINT, an autoencoder whose latent bottleneck is structured as a set of soft, axis-aligned interval memberships learned end-to-end directly from raw numerical data, without any discretization or binarization. Each latent unit corresponds to a human-readable hyper-rectangle in feature space; an instance is encoded by how strongly it falls inside each interval relative to the other units, and its reconstruction error is the anomaly score. This keeps the power of differentiable representation learning while exposing an inspectable internal structure. We make the inductive bias precise: a certified reconstruction-error lower bound for points that fall outside every active coordinate of the learned support (with a Lipschitz-enforced decoder), and a graded, empirically verified suppression mechanism for the usual case in which only a few features are abnormal; and we provide a closed-form, label-free importance that ranks each (unit, feature) pair from quantities the model already maintains, turning trained intervals into auditable candidate constraints without ever seeing an anomaly label. On 48 ADBench benchmarks against 22 baselines under a common [-1, 1]-normalized protocol, DIFFINT attains the best mean rank overall on both metrics (4.10 on ROC-AUC, 4.16 on AUPR); among inlier-only detectors it leads its regime clearly, and it is competitive with the strongest contaminated-data detectors (see the stratified and complete-case analyses). It is the only interpretable detector in the statistically-tied leading cluster of seven methods.

</details>


### [249] [Beyond Endpoint Scores: Time- and Capacity-Conditioned Evaluation of Continual Knowledge Updating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03900)
*Heejin Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出持续知识更新方法仅凭单一最终检查点和单一传统适配器秩的比较是不充分的。通过在24个月Wikidata流上比较周期层次结构与累积回放，并变动评估月份、LoRA秩和查询措辞，发现"赢家"随这些参数变化而改变——周期层次结构在低秩回放时领先5.0分，在高秩回放时反而落后11.6分，时间平均评估下差距可达9-13分。作者因此建议报告轨迹和能力扫描曲线，仅当排序在评估区域内稳定时才宣布稳健赢家，否则应报告赢家区域和保留-稳定性-成本前沿。在此协议下，周期层次结构只是低更新成本的操作点，而非质量赢家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续知识更新方法比较往往只依赖单一最终检查点和一个传统适配器秩来判定优劣，作者质疑这种评估方式可能不足以识别真正更好的操作点，因为方法排名可能随评估时间和回放侧适配能力的变化而改变。

Method: 在Qwen2.5-1.5B和Llama-3.2-1B上，针对24个月的Wikidata流数据，将固定周期层次结构与累积回放基线进行比较。系统地变动评估月份、回放LoRA秩（如8到72）、查询措辞以及是否使用保留的改写句，并考察不同评估协议（单点评估vs时间平均评估、整合对齐端点）下的性能表现。

Result: 发现赢家随评估区域参数变动而反转：在Qwen2.5-1.5B上，层次结构对秩-8回放有5.0点优势，但对秩-72回放却落后11.6点；高秩下整合对齐端点可能显示平局，而时间平均回放领先9-13点。该秩条件反转在Llama-3.2-1B和保留改写句上也复现。

Conclusion: 持续更新中的方法排名取决于性能测量时间与回放侧适配容量的联合作用。应报告轨迹和能力扫描，仅在排序稳定时才宣称稳健赢家；否则应指明赢家区域并报告保留-稳定性-成本前沿。在此协议下，周期层次结构应定位为低更新成本的操作点，而非质量上的胜者。

Abstract: Continual knowledge-updating methods are often declared superior from one final checkpoint and one conventional adapter rank. We show that this can be insufficient to identify the better operating point. Holding a periodic hierarchy fixed, we compare it with cumulative replay over a 24-month Wikidata stream while varying evaluation month, replay LoRA rank, and query formulation. The apparent winner changes across this region: on Qwen2.5-1.5B, the hierarchy's 5.0-point advantage over rank-8 replay becomes an 11.6-point deficit against rank-72 replay, and at high ranks a consolidation-aligned endpoint can suggest a tie while time-averaged replay leads by 9-13 points. The same rank-conditioned reversal appears on Llama-3.2-1B and held-out paraphrases.
  These results show that method ranking in continual updating can depend jointly on when performance is measured and how much replay-side adaptation capacity the baseline receives. We therefore propose reporting trajectories and capacity sweeps, and declaring a robust winner only when the ordering is stable across the evaluation region; otherwise, comparisons should report winner regions and retention-stability-cost frontiers. Under this protocol, the periodic hierarchy is a lower-update-cost operating point, not a quality winner.

</details>


### [250] [RATL: Learning from Retrieved Residuals for Robust Multivariate Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03937)
*Yuchen He,Yueyang Cang,Zhiyuan Ning,Ningyu Wang,Li Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RATL，一种用于多元时间序列预测的即插即用残差检索与反馈校正方法。它冻结基础预测器，将历史预测残差作为仅训练内存，推理时检索相似历史上下文的残差轨迹，并通过集合感知路由器选择和组合这些轨迹来校正基础预测，在大多数实验中提升了冻结基础预测器的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成(RAG)在语言模型中效果显著，但直接将其思想用于连续输出回归（如时间序列预测）时，直接复用检索到的目标值往往不稳健，因为样本在输出水平、数值尺度或局部动态上存在差异。此外，传统预测流程虽使用残差进行优化和误差诊断，但并未将历史残差实例作为可检索记忆在推理时访问。

Method: RATL包含三个关键设计：(1)冻结基础预测器以构造检索键，将其历史预测残差转化为特定于该基础模型的仅训练内存；(2)推理时在因果可用性约束下检索与当前上下文相似的历史残差轨迹；(3)使用作用于预测块和变量的集合感知路由器来选择并组合这些残差轨迹，同时通过验证集上的校正强度选择来限制残差过度注入。

Result: 在真实世界基准上，以iTransformer为主要冻结基础预测器，RATL在大多数实验设置中提升了冻结基础预测器的性能，且优于多个强预测基线；消融实验显示学习路由增强了原始残差反馈，验证集校正强度选择防止了残差过度注入，并验证了跨骨干网络的迁移性。

Conclusion: RATL将检索对象从历史目标值转变为基础模型特定的历史预测误差，为连续输出预测提供了基于残差记忆的即插即用学习反馈校正范式，开辟了检索增强在时间序列预测中的新路径。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) complements parametric models with retrieved external evidence. The same idea is attractive for continuous-output regression, but directly reusing retrieved target values is often not robust when samples differ in output level, numerical scale, or local dynamics. Moreover, conventional forecasting pipelines generally use residuals for model optimization and error diagnosis, but do not retain individual historical residual examples as memory that can be accessed at inference time.For multivariate time-series forecasting, we propose RATL, a plug-in residual-retrieval and feedback-correction method. RATL freezes a base forecaster to construct retrieval keys and turns its historical forecast residuals into a train-only memory specific to that base model. At inference time, RATL retrieves residual trajectories from similar historical contexts subject to causal availability constraints, then uses a set-aware router operating over forecast blocks and variables to select and combine these trajectories. Experiments show that historical residuals matched to the current context contain reusable forecasting information and that RATL improves frozen base forecasters in most experimental settings. Ablations further show that learned routing strengthens raw residual feedback, while validation-based correction-strength selection limits residual over-injection.On real-world benchmarks, we use iTransformer as the primary frozen base forecaster, compare against multiple strong forecasting baselines, and test transferability across backbones. The results show that RATL can further improve base-forecaster performance in most settings.Overall, RATL shifts the retrieved object from historical target values to base-model-specific historical forecast errors, providing a plug-in, residual-memory-based paradigm for learned feedback correction in continuous-output forecasting.

</details>


### [251] [Headroom-Drift Replay: A Primitive for Principled Replay Control in GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03941)
*Hyun Bin Park,Du-Seong Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Headroom-Drift Replay，一种用于GRPO的组级回放选择控制原语，通过将回放决策分解为Headroom（按剩余学习价值排序存储组）和Drift（按与当前策略兼容性筛选）两个步骤，在不增加辅助生成或训练机制的情况下，显著提升基于RL的后训练效率，尤其在智能体环境中大幅降低墙钟时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于强化学习的推理模型后训练日益受限于重复的fresh rollout生成开销，尤其在智能体场景中环境交互占据主导的墙钟成本。现有回放方法通常嵌入到包含探索、经验重构或混合策略优化的大型训练管线中，使得回放本身贡献难以被孤立评估。作者希望回答一个聚焦问题：仅靠原则性的回放选择本身能走多远？

Method: 提出Headroom-Drift Replay：一个组级回放控制原语，将回放复用分解为两个独立决策——Headroom负责根据存储组的剩余学习价值进行排序，Drift则依据当前策略的兼容性对组进行门控筛选。fresh on-policy流保持不变，整个方法不引入任何辅助生成或额外训练机制。

Result: 在数学推理、多模态推理和Agentic Search基准上，这一单一干预优于朴素回放，并在Avg Mean@32上匹配或超越更复杂的回放方法。在环境交互主导成本的Agentic Search中，该方法以显著更低的墙钟时间实现了可比质量。

Conclusion: 仅通过原则性的回放选择（Headroom-Drift）即可在不引入复杂训练管线的前提下有效提升RL后训练效率，尤其在智能体环境等交互成本高昂的场景中，该方法在保持质量的同时大幅降低计算开销，证明了简单而精心设计的回放选择策略具有独立且显著的实用价值。

Abstract: RL-based post-training for reasoning models is increasingly bottlenecked by repeated fresh rollout generation, particularly in agentic settings where environment interaction dominates wall-clock cost. Replay can reduce this burden by reusing past trajectories, but existing methods typically embed it within larger training pipelines involving exploration, experience restructuring, or mixed-policy optimization. This makes replay's own contribution difficult to isolate. We ask a focused question: how far can principled replay selection alone go? We introduce Headroom-Drift Replay, a group-level replay control primitive for GRPO that separates reuse into two decisions. Headroom ranks stored groups by remaining learning value, while Drift gates them by compatibility with the current policy. The fresh on-policy stream remains unchanged, and the method adds no auxiliary generation or training machinery. Across mathematical reasoning, multimodal reasoning, and Agentic Search benchmarks, this single intervention outperforms naive replay and matches or exceeds broader replay methods on Avg Mean@32. In Agentic Search, where environment interaction dominates cost, it delivers comparable quality at materially lower wall-clock time.

</details>


### [252] [VestigeKV: The NoPE-MLA KV Cache Carries Its Own Eviction Signal in a Vestigial Branch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03949)
*WenJie Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VestigeKV针对长寿命KV缓存的压缩问题，提出利用NoPE模型中由RoPE残迹重构成的解耦分支（decoupled branch）作为查询无关的显著性信号。通过读取每行11%的数据将缓存分区：top-m行保留在受关注层级，其余行精确移动到GPU常驻归档区（可被认证触发器按步访问），无需训练、量化或修改权重/内核。在Kimi Linear模型上，8倍压缩下检索保持1.00，32倍下为0.92，128倍下标准配置仍达1.00。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期KV缓存必须在查询存在之前被压缩，而已有的基于观测注意力的选择方法（H2O、SnapKV）在此场景失效（在NoPE MLA模型上needle检索仅0.00-0.33），因为token的重要性尚未被观测到。需要一个查询无关的压缩信号。

Method: 利用Kimi Linear中NoPE训练将RoPE残迹（64维解耦分支）重构成显著性通道作为查询无关信号。每行读取11%数据来分区缓存：top-m行留在受关注层级，其余行精确移至GPU常驻归档区，通过认证触发器按步骤可达。提供host offload作为回收VRAM的变体。

Result: 8倍压缩下检索保持1.00，32倍下0.92（8k到65k上下文零差距于全行选择）；受关注层级仅占每token缓存8.1KB的0.25KB（32倍时）；归档区保持bit-exact且GPU常驻；标准配置在128倍下保持1.00。NoPE模型中top-1目标覆盖2.3-6.7%的token（RoPE下为10.2-46.8%），RoPE下相同算子仅0.08。

Conclusion: 查询无关的显著性信号只在无旋转（NoPE）情况下存在，RoPE下查询全能的精确合并被证明不可能。作者表示方法可能扩展到使用NoPE Gated-MLA变体的Kimi K3（若其缓存布局匹配），但仅对已测模型做出声明。

Abstract: The problem. A long-lived KV cache must be compressed before the queries that will read it exist; selection by observed attention (H2O, SnapKV) collapses there (0.00-0.33 needle retrieval on a NoPE MLA model), because a token's importance has not yet been observed. The method. On Kimi Linear, VestigeKV evicts by a query-independent signal the cache already carries: the 64-dimensional decoupled branch, a vestige of RoPE that NoPE training repurposes into a salience channel. Reading 11% of each row, it partitions the cache: the top-m rows stay in the attended tier; every other row moves -- exactly, never deleted -- to a GPU-resident archive reachable per step by a certified trigger. No training, no quantization, no weight or kernel change. Cost. Nothing measurable: retrieval holds at 1.00 under 8x and 0.92 under 32x from 8k to 65k context, zero gap to full-row selection. The attended tier is 0.25 KB of Kimi Linear's 8.1 KB per-token cache at 32x; the archive stays bit-exact and GPU-resident, with host offload as the VRAM-reclaiming variant. The recall tier -- the standard configuration -- holds 128x at 1.00. Kimi K3 is reported to use a NoPE Gated-MLA variant; if its cache layout matches, the method plausibly extends there -- we make no claim beyond the measured model. NoPE exclusivity. The identical operator on a RoPE MLA collapses to 0.08 (plain eviction: 0.42); query-independent salience itself exists only without rotation (top-1 targets span 2.3-6.7% of tokens vs. 10.2-46.8%), and query-universal exact merging is provably impossible under RoPE. All thresholds were frozen before data; 20 archived verdicts and 8 closed routes accompany the paper.

</details>


### [253] [OSR: Output Space Redistribution for Adaptive Label Removal in Classification Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03972)
*Minyi Peng,Darian Gunamardi,Ivan Tjuawinata,Yongsen Zheng,Kwok-Yan Lam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于输出空间统计重分布的标签移除方法。该方法作为一个模块化输出过滤器，通过近似重训练模型在标签移除后的置信度向量，无需依赖原始数据、特征空间调整或损失函数收敛，从而缓解了计算开销、隐私问题及可扩展性限制。实验表明该方法在多项分类任务上与完全重训练性能相当，同时具备更高的计算效率和隐私保护能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分类系统不断演进的分类体系中，标签移除是常见需求（如动态更新或消除类别）。现有解决方案（基于重训练和基于特征空间调整）存在共同缺陷：依赖原始数据访问、计算与存储成本高、结果不一致、可扩展性差、模型效用退化。因此需要一种更高效、隐私友好且可扩展的标签移除方法。

Method: 提出一种利用输出空间统计重分布的方法，用于近似重训练模型在标签移除后的置信度向量。该方法可作为模块化输出过滤器部署，避免了特征空间调整或损失函数收敛的计算负担；仅需现有标签和先验输出置信度，无需访问原始数据，从而缓解隐私问题并提升可扩展性。

Result: 在多个分类任务上的广泛实验表明，该方法在性能上与完全重训练方法具有竞争力，同时在计算效率和隐私保护方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 该方法为分类演化场景下的标签移除提供了一种轻量级、隐私友好的替代方案，较传统重训练方法在效率和隐私上具有明显优势，且性能损失可控。

Abstract: Label removal occurs frequently in classification systems with evolving taxonomies, where categories must be dynamically updated or eliminated. To accommodate such changes, classification models must adapt accordingly. Existing solutions, broadly categorized as retraining-based and feature-space-adjustment-based, share common limitations despite their variations, including reliance on access to original data, substantial computational and storage costs, inconsistent results, poor scalability, and degradation of model utility. To address this, we propose a novel approach that leverages statistical redistribution in the output space to approximate the post-removal confidence vectors of a retrained model. Applicable as a modular output filter, our method bypasses the burden of feature-space adjustments or loss-function convergence, alleviating scalability limitations. Furthermore, by requiring only existing labels and prior output confidences, the method potentially mitigates privacy concerns inherent to data-dependent solutions. Extensive experiments demonstrate competitive performance against full retraining, with improvements in computational efficiency and privacy preservation across several classification tasks.

</details>


### [254] [RobustSeiz: An Open-Source Framework for Benchmarking the Robustness of EEG Seizure Detection Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04007)
*Mohammad Mohammadi,Alireza Zarei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RobustSeiz，一个开源、模型无关的框架，用于在部署前对癫痫检测器进行标准化、可复现的压力测试，覆盖真实世界的采集变异性、伪影和对抗性输入，超越传统干净数据上的准确率评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癫痫检测器在留出数据上表现良好，但在真实世界采集变异性、伪影和对抗性输入下可能失效，现有评估缺乏标准化、可复现的压力测试协议。

Method: 将四个公开头皮EEG数据集（CHB-MIT、TUSZ、Siena、SeizeIT1）标准化为BIDS-EEG结构；在预定义超参数网格上扫描环境、噪声和对抗性变换；以受试者独立方式评估；提供Docker化GPU流水线、实验注册表、完整评估和研究子集模式；报告事件级与样本级多种指标及Monte Carlo dropout预测一致性。

Result: 使用当代癫痫检测器在TUSZ上演示了完整变换网格；AWGN分析展示了扰动严重程度如何影响检测质量、发作起始时间和预测一致性。

Conclusion: RobustSeiz提供了共享的基准测试标准，用于在真实临床压力源下评估癫痫检测器的稳健性，将部署前评估从干净数据准确率扩展到更全面的鲁棒性验证。

Abstract: Despite strong performance on held-out electroencephalography (EEG) data, seizure detectors may fail under real-world acquisition variability, artifacts, and adversarial inputs. We introduce RobustSeiz, an open-source, model-agnostic framework that provides a standardized, reproducible protocol for stress-testing and comparing seizure detectors under controlled, clinically motivated distribution shifts before deployment. We standardize four public scalp-EEG corpora (CHB-MIT, TUSZ, Siena, and SeizeIT1) into BIDS-EEG trees and evaluate subject-independent detectors on held-out splits. Environment, noise, and adversarial transforms are swept over predefined hyperparameter grids. Each run reports sample- and event-level sensitivity, precision, F1, false positives per 24 h, Lead and Lag onset timing, and Monte Carlo dropout predictive agreement. RobustSeiz includes a Dockerized GPU pipeline, experiment registry, and full-evaluation and research-subset modes. We demonstrate the framework with a contemporary seizure detector on TUSZ across the complete implemented shift grid; an AWGN analysis illustrates how perturbation severity changes detection quality, onset timing, and predictive agreement. RobustSeiz provides a shared benchmarking standard for evaluating seizure-detector robustness under realistic clinical stressors, extending pre-deployment assessment beyond clean-data accuracy.

</details>


### [255] [Unlocking Lossless Speedups in LLMs via Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04010)
*Subham Sekhar Sahoo,Lingjie Chen,Khiem Pham,Jonathan Geuter,Chaitanya Dwivedi,Varad Pimpalkhute,Yash Akhauri,Alexander Moreno,Mikhail Yurochkin,Zhenting Wang,Mostafa Elhoushi,Nolan Dey,Shane Bergsma,Joel Hestness,John Thickstun,Eric Xing,Zhengzhong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Uno，一种扩散增强的LLM新范式：通过将模型参数解耦为AR权重和轻量扩散权重，在保持AR模型原有分布质量的同时，利用扩散并行生成多个token，实现无损加速和推理时扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM依赖自回归逐token生成，存在严重推理速度瓶颈；现有投机解码需要额外草稿模型，而扩散LLM（d-LLMs）会牺牲底层AR模型的质量。作者希望在不牺牲质量、无需额外草稿模型的前提下，实现并行token生成加速。

Method: 提出扩散增强LLM类别，将参数解耦为：AR权重（标准NTP目标训练）和轻量扩散权重（通过对蒸馏阶段学习，实现多token并行生成）。引入Ψ-Spec采样器族，支持在固定上下文长度下无损加速和推理时扩展。模型Uno可从零训练或由现有开源AR LLM增强而来。

Result: Uno在所有评估批次大小上吞吐量均优于领先的投机解码方法，相对基础AR模型最高获得3倍加速（含设备支持的最大批次）。8B Uno在智能体工具使用、编码和长上下文推理基准上超越26B DiffusionGemma和专有Mercury 2。

Conclusion: Uno成功弥合了扩散去噪LLM与AR LLM之间的差距：既保留AR模型的分布质量，又利用扩散实现高效并行生成，展示了扩散增强方法在实际推理加速中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) owe much of their success to next-token prediction (NTP), but their autoregressive (AR) structure requires slow, sequential token generation. To overcome this bottleneck, we introduce diffusion-augmented LLMs, a new class of models that defines an AR model distribution while using diffusion to draw multiple tokens in parallel from that distribution. We decouple the parameters of these models into two sets: AR weights, trained using the standard NTP objective, and lightweight diffusion weights, trained to generate multiple tokens simultaneously. The diffusion weights are learned through a simple Diffusion Distillation phase that adds negligible overhead to existing LLM training pipelines. We also introduce $Ψ$-Spec, a family of samplers that enables lossless acceleration and inference-time scaling at a fixed context length. Unlike speculative decoding, our method requires no separate draft model. Unlike diffusion LLMs (d-LLMs), it accelerates generation without sacrificing the quality of the underlying AR model. The resulting models, called Uno, can be trained from scratch or built by augmenting existing open-weight AR LLMs. Uno achieves higher throughput than leading speculative-decoding methods at every evaluated batch size and delivers up to $3\times$ speedups over the base AR model, including at the largest batch size supported by the device. Notably, our 8B Uno model outperforms the leading open d-LLM, the 26B DiffusionGemma, and the proprietary Mercury 2 across all evaluated benchmarks in agentic tool use, coding, and long-context reasoning. We release code and checkpoints at: https://s-sahoo.github.io/uno/

</details>


### [256] [A location-invariant estimator of extremal quantile treatment effects for heavy-tailed distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04018)
*Xin Yu,Shuwei Huang,Jicheng Liu,Jielin Tang,Bolin Wang,Yunxiao Zhang,Tian Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了位置不变的极端分位数处理效应（QTE）估计方法。针对重尾潜在结果变量，现有极端QTE估计器依赖外推和因果极端值指数（EVI）估计，但该估计器在潜在结果分布发生共同位置平移时不具有不变性。作者通过两步解决：1）将位置不变的Fraga EVI估计器通过逆倾向得分加权适配到因果场景；2）用基于差分的方案替代原始外推公式，使位置参数在分位数差分时抵消。最终提出位置不变的极端QTE估计器，并建立了其一致性和渐近正态性，提供一致方差估计量用于有效推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际应用中，目标分位数常远离观测数据范围（极端分位数），研究治疗对重尾结果分布极值处的影响具有重要价值。但现有的极端QTE估计器缺乏位置不变性——即对潜在结果分布进行共同平移时，估计值会变化，而总体QTE本身是位置不变的，这是理论上的不一致缺陷。

Method: 两步法：(1) 使用逆倾向得分加权（IPSW）将原本观测场景下位置不变的Fraga EVI估计器适配到因果推断框架；(2) 将传统外推公式替换为基于差分的外推方案，使位置参数在分位数做差时自然抵消，从而保证估计器的位置不变性。

Result: 建立了所提极端QTE估计器的一致性（consistency）和渐近正态性（asymptotic normality），并提供了相应的方差一致估计量以进行渐近有效推断。模拟研究验证了位置不变性、对阈值的稳定性以及置信区间的覆盖概率。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了极端QTE估计器的位置不变性问题，在理论性质（一致性、渐近正态性）上得到保证，且模拟实验证明其在实际表现上具有位置不变、阈值稳定和良好推断覆盖等优点，为重尾结果下极端分位数因果效应推断提供了更可靠的工具。

Abstract: Quantile treatment effects (QTEs) measure the effect of a treatment on the distribution of an outcome, and their estimation at extreme quantile levels is of central interest in applications where the target quantiles lie far beyond the range of the data. For heavy-tailed potential outcomes, existing extremal QTE estimators rely on extrapolation combined with a causal extreme value index (EVI) estimator, but the resulting estimator is not invariant under a common location shift of the potential outcome distributions, even though the population QTE is. We address this issue in two steps. First, we adapt the location-invariant Fraga estimator of the EVI to the causal setting using inverse propensity score weighting. Second, we replace the original extrapolation formula with a difference-based scheme, under which the location parameter cancels when quantile differences are taken. The resulting QTE estimator is therefore location invariant. We establish the consistency and asymptotic normality of the proposed extremal QTE estimators, and provide a consistent variance estimator, leading to asymptotically valid inference. A simulation study confirms the location invariance, the stability with respect to the threshold, and the coverage of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [257] [Subspace Inference Enables Efficient Active Reward Learning from Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04066)
*Yutai Zhou,Erdem Bıyık*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PreferenceEKF，一种样本高效的主动偏好学习方法，通过将主动偏好学习框架化为序贯贝叶斯滤波问题，在低维参数子空间中使用扩展卡尔曼滤波追踪奖励模型的不确定性，从而实现可扩展的神经参数采样和高效采集函数计算。在D4RL和V-D4RL基准上，该方法在样本效率、运行时间、可扩展性和校准方面优于其他贝叶斯深度学习方法，且学到的奖励模型能带来有竞争力的离线强化学习策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF中的奖励模型学习样本效率低下，主动学习是关键，但大型神经网络奖励模型的有效不确定性量化是核心挑战，因为对全网络参数空间做后验推断计算上不可行。

Method: 将主动偏好学习建模为序贯贝叶斯滤波问题，在低维参数子空间内通过扩展卡尔曼滤波器（EKF）进行序贯推断，持续更新奖励模型后验，实现对神经网络参数的规模化采样以高效计算主动学习的采集函数。

Result: 在D4RL和V-D4RL基准上，PreferenceEKF在样本效率、运行时间、可扩展性和校准方面优于其他贝叶斯深度学习方法，且学到的奖励模型导出的策略在离线强化学习上具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了可扩展贝叶斯方法在RLHF偏好奖励建模中的潜力，为解决主动学习中的不确定性量化难题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) has emerged as a powerful yet sample-inefficient approach for learning reward models from human preferences, making active learning a critical component in synthesizing informative preference queries. However, effective uncertainty quantification required for active learning remains a key challenge for large neural network reward models. In this paper, we introduce PreferenceEKF, a sample-efficient approach that tracks reward model uncertainty by framing active preference learning as a sequential Bayesian filtering problem. Instead of relying on computationally prohibitive posterior inference over the full neural network parameter space, our method performs sequential inference via an extended Kalman filter within a low-dimensional parameter subspace, continuously updating the reward model posterior as new preference queries arrive. Our approach enables scalable sampling of neural network parameters to efficiently compute acquisition functions for active reward learning. Experiments on the D4RL and V-D4RL benchmarks demonstrate that our approach achieves better sample efficiency, runtime, scalability, and calibration compared to other Bayesian deep learning approaches, and the learned reward models lead to competitive offline reinforcement learning policy performance. This highlights the potential of scalable Bayesian methods for preference-based reward modeling in RLHF. Our code is available at https://github.com/yutaizhou/bnn_pref.

</details>


### [258] [Conditioning Degenerate Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04090)
*Uğur Aydın,Tamer Başar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用因果最优传输来为具有奇异扩散系数的条件生成扩散模型定义近似损失函数，在底层条件密度不存在或不光滑的情况下，识别最小熵控制以实现引导，所需假设最少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前条件生成模型严重依赖得分函数进行训练引导，但当扩散过程具有奇异扩散系数，且底层条件密度不存在或不光滑时，传统的得分函数方法会失效，需要更通用的框架来处理这类情况。
